1. **I1 — Enabled tag:** A module is enabled on a host only through a tag that the host enables, unless the host's own configuration enables it directly.
2. **I2 — Supported platform:** A module is enabled on a host only on a platform that supports it and, unless the host's own configuration enables it directly, only because that platform supports it.
3. **I3 — Tag override:** A tag override applies only to a module that its tag enables.
4. **I4 — Enabled realization:** A responsibility is realized only by an enabled module.

   when ?module is tag-enabled on ?host
   and ?module is enabled on ?host by default through ?tag
   and ?tag sets ?path to ?value for ?module
+  and not (?tag == browsers and ?module == firefox_001)
+
+rule browsers-firefox-override: ?host gets myConfig.modules.firefox_001.default set to ?value by firefox_001
+  when firefox_001 is tag-enabled on ?host
+  and firefox_001 is enabled on ?host
+  and browsers sets myConfig.modules.firefox_001.default to ?value for firefox_001
 
 requirements for tags
   every ?host, ?module, ?tag where ?host adds ?module to ?tag: ?module can opt into ?tag
   every ?host, ?module, ?tag where ?host adds ?module to ?tag: ?module is supported on ?host
   every ?host, ?module where ?host turns on ?module: not ?host turns off ?module
